Motivation\n\n| Challenge                              | Why CQRS/Event-Sourcing?                                                     |\n|----------------------------------------|-----------------------------------------------------------------------------|\n| Multi-currency, social payment flows   | Immutable events preserve precise FX rates + social context at the moment of intent. |\n| Regulatory audit (KYC/AML, GDPR)       | Full event log serves as the system of record—no mutable state hidden from auditors. |\n| Gamified reputation & trust signals    | Reputation scores are projections computed from the same source-of-truth event stream. |\n| Feature agility                        | New read models (e.g., “top donors this week”) can be generated without writing migrations. |\n\n---\n\n## 2. Ubiquitous Language\n\n| Term          | Meaning                                                        |\n|---------------|----------------------------------------------------------------|\n| CrowdPod      | Autonomous wallet + rule engine owned by a social group.       |\n| Member        | User admitted to a CrowdPod with a **Role** (Admin, Payer …).  |\n| Command       | “Intent” to change state – must be validated.                  |\n| Event         | Successfully validated state change – **immutable**.           |\n| Aggregate     | Consistency boundary on the write side (e.g., `CrowdPod`).     |\n\n---\n\n## 3. High-level Component Diagram\n\n```mermaid\nflowchart LR\n  subgraph Write [Write (Command) Side]\n    C[Command API<br/>GraphQL / gRPC] -->|1| V[Validator]\n    V -->|2| AGG[Aggregates<br/>Domain Logic]\n    AGG -->|3| ES[(Event Store<br/>PostgreSQL<br/>w/ WAL + logical replication)]\n  end\n\n  subgraph Infra  [\"Streaming Backbone\"]\n    ES ==>|Logical Replication Slot| K[Kafka Topic:<br/>crowdpay.events]\n  end\n\n  subgraph Read [Read (Query) Side]\n    K -->|4| PROJ[Projections<br/>Materialisers]\n    PROJ -->|5| RM[(Read Models<br/>ElasticSearch, Redis, ClickHouse)]\n    RM -->|6| QueryAPI[REST / GraphQL]\n  end\n\n  AGG -->|7| SagaCmd[Outbox → Kafka (saga.*)]\n  SagaCmd -->|8| SagaOrch[[Saga Orchestrator]]\n  SagaOrch -->|9| ExternalSvc[/FX Engine, KYC, AML/]\n```\n\nLegend:\n\n1. Command received.  \n2. Validation + invariant checks.  \n3. Events appended atomically inside Postgres transaction.  \n4. Events copied to Kafka via logical decoding plugin (`wal2json`).  \n5. Projectors consume Kafka to update denormalised views.  \n6. Queries hit fast, isolated read stores.  \n7. Aggregates emit “side-effect” commands through an _outbox_ table.  \n8. Saga orchestrator coordinates long-running workflows.  \n9. External micro-services participate via compensating actions.\n\n---\n\n## 4. Event Store Specification\n\n- **Physical store**: Partitioned PostgreSQL 15 + ZFS.  \n- **Schema**:\n\n```sql\nCREATE TABLE crowdpay.event_store (\n    stream_id     UUID      NOT NULL,\n    stream_type   TEXT      NOT NULL,            -- e.g. 'CrowdPod'\n    version       INTEGER   NOT NULL,            -- optimistic locking\n    event_id      UUID      PRIMARY KEY,\n    event_type    TEXT      NOT NULL,\n    event_data    JSONB     NOT NULL,\n    meta_data     JSONB     NOT NULL,            -- user-id, ip, kyc_level …\n    created_at    TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT now()\n);\n\nCREATE UNIQUE INDEX ux_stream_version\n  ON crowdpay.event_store (stream_id, version);\n```\n\n- **Guarantees**:  \n  • Append-only.  \n  • Serializable isolation.  \n  • SNAPSHOT EXPORT every 24 h for cold storage (S3 + Glacier).\n\n---\n\n## 5. Command-Side Implementation (Python 3.11)\n\nBelow is a trimmed version of the production library used by the **Command API** service.\n\n```python\n\"\"\"\ncrowdpay_connect.commanding\n~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~\nCore CQRS command-side primitives.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport uuid\nimport json\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass, asdict\nfrom datetime import datetime, timezone\nfrom typing import Protocol, Sequence, Any\n\nimport psycopg  # psycopg3\nfrom psycopg.rows import class_row\n\n\n# ---------- Domain Events -------------------------------------------------- #\n\nclass DomainEvent(Protocol):\n    event_type: str\n    occurred_at: datetime\n\n    def to_json(self) -> str: ...\n    @classmethod\n    def from_row(cls, row: Any) -> \"DomainEvent\": ...\n\n\n@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)\nclass CrowdPodCreated:\n    pod_id: str\n    owner_id: str\n    currency: str\n    name: str\n    event_type: str = \"CrowdPodCreated\"\n    occurred_at: datetime = datetime.now(timezone.utc)\n\n    def to_json(self) -> str:\n        return json.dumps(asdict(self), default=str)\n\n\n@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)\nclass FundsDeposited:\n    pod_id: str\n    member_id: str\n    amount_minor: int\n    currency: str\n    tx_ref: str\n    event_type: str = \"FundsDeposited\"\n    occurred_at: datetime = datetime.now(timezone.utc)\n\n    def to_json(self) -> str:\n        return json.dumps(asdict(self), default=str)\n\n\n# ---------- Command Objects ------------------------------------------------ #\n\n@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)\nclass CreateCrowdPod:\n    owner_id: str\n    currency: str\n    name: str\n\n\n@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)\nclass DepositFunds:\n    pod_id: str\n    member_id: str\n    amount_minor: int\n    currency: str\n    tx_ref: str\n\n\n# ---------- Exceptions ----------------------------------------------------- #\n\nclass ConcurrencyError(RuntimeError):\n    pass\n\n\nclass ValidationError(ValueError):\n    pass\n\n\n# ---------- Aggregate Root ------------------------------------------------- #\n\nclass CrowdPodAggregate:\n    \"\"\"\n    In-memory representation reconstructed from events.\n    \"\"\"\n    def __init__(self, pod_id: str | None = None) -> None:\n        self.id: str | None = pod_id\n        self.owner_id: str | None = None\n        self.currency: str | None = None\n        self.name: str | None = None\n        self.balance_minor: int = 0\n        self._version: int = 0\n        self._pending: list[DomainEvent] = []\n\n    # --- Rehydration -------------------------------------------------------\n\n    @classmethod\n    def load(cls, events: Sequence[DomainEvent]) -> \"CrowdPodAggregate\":\n        agg = cls()\n        for ev in events:\n            agg._apply(ev, is_new=False)\n            agg._version += 1\n        return agg\n\n    # --- Command Handlers --------------------------------------------------\n\n    def handle(self, cmd: Any) -> None:\n        match cmd:\n            case CreateCrowdPod(owner_id=oid, currency=cur, name=nm):\n                if self.id is not None:\n                    raise ValidationError(\"CrowdPod already exists.\")\n                event = CrowdPodCreated(\n                    pod_id=str(uuid.uuid4()),\n                    owner_id=oid,\n                    currency=cur,\n                    name=nm,\n                )\n                self._record(event)\n\n            case DepositFunds(pod_id=_pid, member_id=mid,\n                              amount_minor=amt, currency=cur, tx_ref=tx):\n                if cur != self.currency:\n                    raise ValidationError(\"Currency mismatch.\")\n                if amt <= 0:\n                    raise ValidationError(\"Invalid amount.\")\n                event = FundsDeposited(\n                    pod_id=self.id,\n                    member_id=mid,\n                    amount_minor=amt,\n                    currency=cur,\n                    tx_ref=tx,\n                )\n                self._record(event)\n\n            case _:\n                raise ValidationError(f\"Unknown command: {cmd}\")\n\n    # --- Event Application -------------------------------------------------\n\n    def _apply(self, event: DomainEvent, *, is_new: bool) -> None:\n        if isinstance(event, CrowdPodCreated):\n            self.id = event.pod_id\n            self.owner_id = event.owner_id\n            self.currency = event.currency\n            self.name = event.name\n        elif isinstance(event, FundsDeposited):\n            self.balance_minor += event.amount_minor\n        else:\n            raise RuntimeError(f\"Unsupported event {event}\")\n\n        if is_new:\n            self._pending.append(event)\n\n    def _record(self, event: DomainEvent) -> None:\n        self._apply(event, is_new=True)\n\n    # --- Public helpers ----------------------------------------------------\n\n    @property\n    def pending_events(self) -> list[DomainEvent]:\n        return self._pending\n\n    def mark_committed(self) -> None:\n        self._pending.clear()\n\n    @property\n    def version(self) -> int:\n        return self._version\n\n\n# ---------- Repository Layer ---------------------------------------------- #\n\nclass CrowdPodRepository:\n    \"\"\"\n    Stores and retrieves aggregates through the event store.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, conn: psycopg.Connection) -> None:\n        self._conn = conn\n\n    def _fetch_stream(self, stream_id: str) -> list[DomainEvent]:\n        cur = self._conn.cursor(row_factory=class_row(CrowdPodCreated))\n        cur.execute(\n            \"SELECT event_type, event_data \"\n            \"FROM crowdpay.event_store \"\n            \"WHERE stream_id = %s \"\n            \"ORDER BY version ASC\",\n            (stream_id,),\n        )\n\n        events = []\n        for event_type, event_data in cur.fetchall():\n            data = json.loads(event_data)\n            if event_type == \"CrowdPodCreated\":\n                events.append(CrowdPodCreated(**data))\n            elif event_type == \"FundsDeposited\":\n                events.append(FundsDeposited(**data))\n            else:\n                continue  # Unknown events are skipped (forward compatibility)\n        return events\n\n    def get(self, stream_id: str) -> CrowdPodAggregate:\n        events = self._fetch_stream(stream_id)\n        if not events:\n            raise ValidationError(\"CrowdPod not found\")\n        return CrowdPodAggregate.load(events)\n\n    def save(self, agg: CrowdPodAggregate) -> None:\n        if not agg.pending_events:\n            return\n\n        with self._conn.transaction():\n            for ev in agg.pending_events:\n                self._conn.execute(\n                    \"INSERT INTO crowdpay.event_store \"\n                    \"(stream_id, stream_type, version, event_id, \"\n                    \" event_type, event_data, meta_data) \"\n                    \"VALUES (%s, %s, %s, %s, %s, %s::jsonb, %s::jsonb)\",\n                    (\n                        agg.id,\n                        \"CrowdPod\",\n                        agg.version + 1,\n                        uuid.uuid4(),\n                        ev.event_type,\n                        ev.to_json(),\n                        json.dumps({\"schema\": 1}),\n                    ),\n                )\n                agg._version += 1\n\n        agg.mark_committed()\n```\n\nThe full implementation includes an **outbox** pattern and robust retry/back-off logic (omitted here for brevity).\n\n---\n\n## 6. Read-Side Projection\n\nRead models are produced by **idempotent** consumers subscribed to the Kafka topic `crowdpay.events`.\n\n```python\n\"\"\"\ncrowdpay_connect.read_model.projectors\n~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~\nElasticSearch projector for quick CrowdPod lookups.\n\"\"\"\nimport json\nimport logging\nfrom typing import Any\n\nfrom elasticsearch import AsyncElasticsearch\nfrom aiokafka import AIOKafkaConsumer\n\nfrom crowdpay_connect.events import deserialise_event  # shared util\n\nlogger = logging.getLogger(__name__)\n\n\nclass CrowdPodSearchProjector:\n    INDEX = \"crowdpod_search_v1\"\n\n    def __init__(self, es: AsyncElasticsearch, bootstrap_servers: str):\n        self._es = es\n        self._consumer = AIOKafkaConsumer(\n            \"crowdpay.events\",\n            bootstrap_servers=bootstrap_servers,\n            group_id=\"crowdpod-search\",\n            value_deserializer=lambda b: json.loads(b.decode()),\n            enable_auto_commit=False,\n        )\n\n    async def run(self) -> None:\n        await self._consumer.start()\n        try:\n            async for msg in self._consumer:\n                evt = deserialise_event(msg.value)\n                await self._handle_event(evt)\n                await self._consumer.commit()\n        except Exception:\n            logger.exception(\"Projection loop crashed!\")\n        finally:\n            await self._consumer.stop()\n\n    # --- Event Handlers ----------------------------------------------------\n\n    async def _handle_event(self, event: Any) -> None:\n        if event[\"event_type\"] == \"CrowdPodCreated\":\n            await self._es.index(\n                index=self.INDEX,\n                id=event[\"pod_id\"],\n                document={\n                    \"name\": event[\"name\"],\n                    \"currency\": event[\"currency\"],\n                    \"owner_id\": event[\"owner_id\"],\n                    \"created_at\": event[\"occurred_at\"],\n                },\n                op_type=\"create\",\n            )\n        elif event[\"event_type\"] == \"FundsDeposited\":\n            await self._es.update(\n                index=self.INDEX,\n                id=event[\"pod_id\"],\n                script={\n                    \"source\": \"ctx._source.balance_minor += params.amt\",\n                    \"params\": {\"amt\": event[\"amount_minor\"]},\n                },\n                retry_on_conflict=5,\n            )\n```\n\nDesign notes:\n\n1. **Exactly-once semantics** are achieved via Kafka offsets + deterministic `id` in ElasticSearch.  \n2. **Backfills**: Replaying the full event history is safe; the script handles duplicate events.\n\n---\n\n## 7. Saga Orchestrator (Settlement Flow)\n\n```python\n\"\"\"\ncrowdpay_connect.saga.settlement\n~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~~\nDistributed Saga to convert multi-currency deposits and\nsettle funds to external banking rails.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport asyncio\nimport logging\nimport uuid\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass\nfrom enum import Enum, auto\n\nfrom crowdpay_connect.bus import publish_command, publish_event\n\nlog = logging.getLogger(__name__)\n\n\nclass SagaState(Enum):\n    INITIATED = auto()\n    FX_RESERVED = auto()\n    BANK_RESERVED = auto()\n    COMPLETED = auto()\n    COMPENSATING = auto()\n    FAILED = auto()\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass SagaContext:\n    saga_id: str\n    pod_id: str\n    amount_minor: int\n    src_currency: str\n    dest_currency: str\n    beneficiary_iban: str\n\n\nclass SettlementSaga:\n    TIMEOUT = 30  # seconds for each step\n\n    def __init__(self, ctx: SagaContext) -> None:\n        self.ctx = ctx\n        self.state = SagaState.INITIATED\n\n    async def execute(self) -> None:\n        try:\n            await self._reserve_fx()\n            await self._reserve_bank_slot()\n            await self._finalise()\n        except Exception as exc:\n            log.warning(\"Saga %s failed: %s – compensating …\", self.ctx.saga_id, exc)\n            await self._compensate()\n            self.state = SagaState.FAILED\n            raise\n\n    async def _reserve_fx(self) -> None:\n        publish_command(\n            \"fx.reserve\",\n            saga_id=self.ctx.saga_id,\n            amount=self.ctx.amount_minor,\n            src=self.ctx.src_currency,\n            dest=self.ctx.dest_currency,\n        )\n        await self._await_event(\"fx.reserved\")\n        self.state = SagaState.FX_RESERVED\n\n    async def _reserve_bank_slot(self) -> None:\n        publish_command(\n            \"bank.reserve\",\n            saga_id=self.ctx.saga_id,\n            amount=self.ctx.amount_minor,\n            currency=self.ctx.dest_currency,\n            iban=self.ctx.beneficiary_iban,\n        )\n        await self._await_event(\"bank.slot_reserved\")\n        self.state = SagaState.BANK_RESERVED\n\n    async def _finalise(self) -> None:\n        publish_command(\"bank.settle\", saga_id=self.ctx.saga_id)\n        await self._await_event(\"bank.settled\")\n        publish_event(\"saga.settlement.completed\", saga_id=self.ctx.saga_id)\n        self.state = SagaState.COMPLETED\n\n    async def _compensate(self) -> None:\n        self.state = SagaState.COMPENSATING\n        publish_command(\"fx.cancel\", saga_id=self.ctx.saga_id)\n        publish_command(\"bank.release\", saga_id=self.ctx.saga_id)\n        await asyncio.gather(\n            self._await_event(\"fx.cancelled\"),\n            self._await_event(\"bank.slot_released\"),\n        )\n        publish_event(\"saga.settlement.compensated\", saga_id=self.ctx.saga_id)\n\n    async def _await_event(self, name: str) -> None:\n        # Pseudo-implementation; production code uses Kafka + Correlation-ID\n        try:\n            await asyncio.wait_for(_event_bus_wait(name, self.ctx.saga_id), timeout=self.TIMEOUT)\n        except asyncio.TimeoutError as exc:\n            raise RuntimeError(f\"Timeout waiting for {name}\") from exc\n\n\ndef start_settlement(\n    pod_id: str,\n    amount_minor: int,\n    src_currency: str,\n    dest_currency: str,\n    beneficiary_iban: str,\n) -> str:\n    saga_id = str(uuid.uuid4())\n    ctx = SagaContext(\n        saga_id=saga_id,\n        pod_id=pod_id,\n        amount_minor=amount_minor,\n        src_currency=src_currency,\n        dest_currency=dest_currency,\n        beneficiary_iban=beneficiary_iban,\n    )\n\n    asyncio.create_task(SettlementSaga(ctx).execute())\n    return saga_id\n```\n\n---\n\n## 8. Compliance & Security by Design\n\n1. **Immutability** – No PII lives in the event payload; sensitive data is encrypted via **envelope encryption** before being stored as `event_data`.  \n2. **Field-level encryption** – KMS-backed keys rotate every 90 days.  \n3. **GDPR Right to Erasure** – Events keep a `subject_id` pointer; “deletion” is processed by cryptographically shredding the CEK (Content Encryption Key).  \n4. **Audit trail** – All event append operations store the `meta_data` → `trace_id`, `source_ip`, `kyc_level`, `bank_id`.  \n5. **Tamper detection** – Merkle root per day signed with server HSM.\n\n---\n\n## 9. Operational Playbook\n\n| Action                               | Cmd / Tooling                                       |\n|--------------------------------------|-----------------------------------------------------|\n| Rebuild read models                  | `python -m crowdpay_connect.tools.replay_events`    |\n| Take snapshot for hot restore        | `SELECT crowdpay.snapshot_make('CrowdPod', ...);`   |\n| Verify merkle integrity              | `crowdpay-cli merkle verify --date 2024-05-30`      |\n| Truncate outbox table (processed)    | Schedule: cron daily via `crowdpay_cleaner`         |\n\n---\n\n## 10. Testing Strategy\n\n1. **Property-based tests** (`hypothesis`) for aggregates guarantee invariants (`balance ≥ 0`).  \n2. **Event fixture contracts** verified through **JSON Schema** in CI.  \n3. **Replay test**: every PR triggers a full replay of prod anon-scrubbed events against head commit; projection checksum must match.\n\n---\n\n### Appendix: Event JSON Schemas (OpenAPI 3.1 extract)\n\n```yaml\nCrowdPodCreated:\n  type: object\n  required: [pod_id, owner_id, currency, name, occurred_at]\n  properties:\n    pod_id:      {type: string, format: uuid}\n    owner_id:    {type: string, format: uuid}\n    currency:    {type: string, enum: [USD, EUR, GBP, NGN, GHS]}\n    name:        {type: string, maxLength: 64}\n    occurred_at: {type: string, format: date-time}\n\nFundsDeposited:\n  type: object\n  required: [pod_id, member_id, amount_minor, currency, tx_ref, occurred_at]\n  properties:\n    pod_id:       {type: string, format: uuid}\n    member_id:    {type: string, format: uuid}\n    amount_minor: {type: integer, minimum: 1}\n    currency:     {type: string}\n    tx_ref:       {type: string}\n    occurred_at:  {type: string, format: date-time}\n```\n\n---\n\n_© 2024 CrowdPay Connect Architecture Guild_\n```","numLines":614,"startLine":1,"totalLines":614}}}

distributed payment.\n\n    The implementation is synchronous for readability, yet compatible with\n    celery / asyncio wrappers because each step is pure I/O.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(\n        self,\n        wallet_service: WalletService,\n        kyc_service: KYCService,\n        risk_service: RiskService,\n        forex_service: ForexService,\n        settlement_service: forex_service\n        self.settlement = settlement_service\n        self.bus = event_bus\n\n    # --------------------------------------------------------------------- #\n    #                          Public entry point                           #\n    # --------------------------------------------------------------------- #\n\n    def execute(self, command: PaymentCommand) -> PaymentResult:\n        \"\"\"\n        Run the saga and return a final PaymentResult—for API serialization.\n\n        Idempotency is expected to be handled by an upstream layer (API GW +\n        database). This coordinator focuses strictly on happy-path/rollback\n        logic.\n        \"\"\"\n        context = SagaContext(command=command)\n        steps = self._build_steps()\n\n        logger.info(\n            \"Starting PaymentSaga: transaction_id=%s, idempotency_key=%s\",\n            context.transaction_id,\n            command.idempotency_key,\n        )\n\n        completed_steps: List[SagaStep] = []\n\n        try:\n            for step in steps:\n                logger.debug(\"Executing step '%s' for transaction %s\", step.name, context.transaction_id)\n                step.action(context)  # Execute main action\n                completed_steps.append(step)\n                logger.debug(\"Step '%s' completed\", step.name)\n        except Exception as exc:\n            logger.warning(\"Step '%s' failed: %s. Triggering compensation.\", step.name, exc)\n            self._compensate(context, reversed(completed_steps))\n            # Persist FAILURE event\n            self.bus.publish(\n                topic=\"payments.lifecycle\",\n                event={\n                    \"event_type\": \"PaymentFailed\",\n                    \"transaction_id\": context.transaction_id,\n                    \"reason\": str(exc),\n                },\n            )\n            return PaymentResult(\n                transaction_id=context.transaction_id,\n                status=PaymentStatus.FAILED,\n                message=str(exc),\n                events=context.events,\n            )\n\n        # ------------------------------  Commit  -------------------------- #\n        self.bus.publish(\n            topic=\"payments.lifecycle\",\n            event={\n                \"event_type\": \"PaymentCompleted\",\n                \"transaction_id\": context.transaction_id,\n            },\n        )\n        return PaymentResult(\n            transaction_id=context.transaction_id,\n            status=PaymentStatus.COMPLETED,\n            events=context.events,\n        )\n\n    # --------------------------------------------------------------------- #\n    #                           Private helpers                             #\n    # --------------------------------------------------------------------- #\n\n    def _build_steps(self) -> List[SagaStep]:\n        \"\"\"\n        Dynamically constructs saga steps; easy to reorder / extend without\n        modifying execute() control flow.\n        \"\"\"\n        return [\n            SagaStep(\n                name=\"kyc_verification\",\n                action=self._action_verify_kyc,\n                # no compensation, nothing to undo\n            ),\n            SagaStep(\n                name=\"risk_assessment\",\n                action=self._action_risk_assessment,\n            ),\n            SagaStep(\n                name=\"debit_payer\",\n                action=self._action_debit_payer,\n                compensation=self._compensate_debit_payer,\n            ),\n            SagaStep(\n                name=\"currency_conversion\",\n                action=self._action_currency_conversion,\n                compensation=self._compensate_currency_conversion,\n            ),\n            SagaStep(\n                name=\"credit_crowdpod\",\n                action=self._action_credit_crowdpod,\n                compensation=self._compensate_credit_crowdpod,\n            ),\n            SagaStep(\n                name=\"settlement\",\n                action=self._action_settlement,\n                compensation=self._compensate_settlement,\n            ),\n        ]\n\n    def _compensate(self, context: SagaContext, steps: List[SagaStep]) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Executes compensation in LIFO order.\"\"\"\n        for step in steps:\n            if step.compensation is None:\n                continue\n            try:\n                logger.debug(\n                    \"Compensating step '%s' for transaction %s\",\n                    step.name,\n                    context.transaction_id,\n                )\n                step.compensation(context)\n                logger.debug(\"Compensation for '%s' successful\", step.name)\n            except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001\n                # Compensation errors are fatal; manual intervention required.\n                logger.error(\n                    \"Compensation for step '%s' failed! Transaction '%s' may \"\n                    \"require manual reconciliation: %s\",\n                    step.name,\n                    context.transaction_id,\n                    exc,\n                )\n                self.bus.publish(\n                    topic=\"payments.lifecycle\",\n                    event={\n                        \"event_type\": \"PaymentCompensationFailed\",\n                        \"transaction_id\": context.transaction_id,\n                        \"failed_step\": step.name,\n                        \"reason\": str(exc),\n                    },\n                )\n                raise\n\n    # --------------------------------------------------------------------- #\n    #                           Saga step actions                           #\n    # --------------------------------------------------------------------- #\n\n    def _action_verify_kyc(self, ctx: SagaContext) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Ensure payer has a valid KYC profile.\"\"\"\n        if not self.kyc.verify_customer(ctx.command.payer_id):\n            raise SagaExecutionError(\"KYC verification failed\")\n        ctx.events.append(\n            {\"event_type\": \"KycVerified\", \"user_id\": ctx.command.payer_id}\n        )\n\n    def _action_risk_assessment(self, ctx: SagaContext) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Invoke real-time transaction risk evaluation.\"\"\"\n        approved = self.risk.assess_payment(\n            user_id=ctx.command.payer_id,\n            amount=ctx.command.amount,\n            currency=ctx.command.source_currency,\n        )\n        if not approved:\n            raise SagaExecutionError(\"Payment declined by risk engine\")\n        ctx.events.append(\n            {\"event_type\": \"PaymentRiskApproved\", \"user_id\": ctx.command.payer_id}\n        )\n\n    def _action_debit_payer(self, ctx: SagaContext) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Debit the payer's wallet.\"\"\"\n        ledger_id = self.wallet.debit(\n            user_id=ctx.command.payer_id,\n            amount=ctx.command.amount,\n            currency=ctx.command.source_currency,\n        )\n        ctx.journal[\"debit_ledger_id\"] = ledger_id\n        ctx.events.append(\n            {\"event_type\": \"WalletDebited\", \"ledger_id\": ledger_id}\n        )\n\n    def _compensate_debit_payer(self, ctx: SagaContext) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Reverse payer debit.\"\"\"\n        ledger_id = self.wallet.credit(\n            user_id=ctx.command.payer_id,\n            amount=ctx.command.amount,\n            currency=ctx.command.source_currency,\n        )\n        ctx.events.append(\n            {\n                \"event_type\": \"WalletDebitReversed\",\n                \"original_ledger_id\": ctx.journal.get(\"debit_ledger_id\"),\n                \"compensation_ledger_id\": ledger_id,\n            }\n        )\n\n    def _action_currency_conversion(self, ctx: SagaContext) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Convert funds if source & target currencies differ.\"\"\"\n        if ctx.command.source_currency == ctx.command.target_currency:\n            ctx.journal[\"converted_amount\"] = ctx.command.amount\n            logger.debug(\"No currency conversion required.\")\n            return\n\n        converted_amount = self.forex.convert(\n            amount=ctx.command.amount,\n            source=ctx.command.source_currency,\n            target=ctx.command.target_currency,\n        )\n        if converted_amount <= 0:\n            raise SagaExecutionError(\"Currency conversion failed\")\n\n        ctx.journal[\"converted_amount\"] = converted_amount\n        ctx.events.append(\n            {\n                \"event_type\": \"CurrencyConverted\",\n                \"from\": ctx.command.source_currency,\n                \"to\": ctx.command.target_currency,\n                \"original_amount\": ctx.command.amount,\n                \"converted_amount\": converted_amount,\n            }\n        )\n\n    def _compensate_currency_conversion(self, ctx: SagaContext) -> None:\n        \"\"\"\n        Currency conversion compensation is effectively a no-op because the\n        actual wallet funds have not left the platform yet—it only informs the\n        ledger entry that conversion was voided. In real-life scenarios, fx\n        markets may require booking a compensating trade.\n        \"\"\"\n        ctx.events.append(\n            {\n                \"event_type\": \"CurrencyConversionVoided\",\n                \"transaction_id\": ctx.transaction_id,\n            }\n        )\n\n    def _action_credit_crowdpod(self, ctx: SagaContext) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Credit funds to the CrowdPod wallet.\"\"\"\n        amount = ctx.journal.get(\"converted_amount\", ctx.command.amount)\n        ledger_id = self.wallet.credit(\n            user_id=ctx.command.crowdpod_id,\n            amount=amount,\n            currency=ctx.command.target_currency,\n        )\n        ctx.journal[\"credit_ledger_id\"] = ledger_id\n        ctx.events.append(\n            {\"event_type\": \"CrowdPodCredited\", \"ledger_id\": ledger_id}\n        )\n\n    def _compensate_credit_crowdpod(self, ctx: SagaContext) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Reverse CrowdPod credit.\"\"\"\n        amount = ctx.journal.get(\"converted_amount\", ctx.command.amount)\n        ledger_id = self.wallet.debit(\n            user_id=ctx.command.crowdpod_id,\n            amount=amount,\n            currency=ctx.command.target_currency,\n        )\n        ctx.events.append(\n            {\n                \"event_type\": \"CrowdPodCreditReversed\",\n                \"original_ledger_id\": ctx.journal.get(\"credit_ledger_id\"),\n                \"compensation_ledger_id\": ledger_id,\n            }\n        )\n\n    def _action_settlement(self, ctx: SagaContext) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Mark transaction as settled in the clearing subsystem.\"\"\"\n        settled = self.settlement.settle_transaction(ctx.transaction_id)\n        if not settled:\n            raise SagaExecutionError(\"Settlement failed\")\n        ctx.events.append(\n            {\"event_type\": \"TransactionSettled\", \"transaction_id\": ctx.transaction_id}\n        )\n\n    def _compensate_settlement(self, ctx: SagaContext) -> None:\n        \"\"\"\n        Settlement compensation is domain-specific; often done via a manual\n        “break” queue. 463→            )\n   464→        logger.debug(\"Commit saga compensation executed for entries: %s\", [e.id for e in self._entries])\n   465→\n   466→\n   467→class _PublishEventsStep:\n   468→    \"\"\"\n   469→    Final saga step that broadcasts domain events. This step is intentionally\n   470→    placed last so that consumers only see COMMITTED entries.\n   471→    \"\"\"\n   472→\n   473→    def __init__(self, publisher: AbstractEventPublisher, *entries: LedgerEntry):\n   474→        self._publisher = publisher\n   475→        self._entries = entries\n   476→        self._published: bool = False\n   477→\n   478→    async def invoke(self) -> None:\n   479→        tasks = [\n   480→            self._publisher.publish(\n   481→                \"ledger.entry.committed\",\n   482→                {\n   483→                    \"entry_id\": str(e.id),\n   484→                    \"wallet_id\": str(e.wallet_id),\n   485→                    \"type\": e.entry_type,\n   486→                    \"amount\": str(e.money.amount),\n   487→                    \"currency\": e.money.currency,\n   488→                    \"external_txn_id\": e.external_txn_id,\n   489→                    \"timestamp\": e.created_at.isoformat(),\n   490→                },\n   491→            )\n   492→            for e in self._entries\n   493→        ]\n   494→        await asyncio.gather(*tasks)\n   495→        self._published = True\n   496→        logger.debug(\"Ledger events published for entries: %s\", [e.id for e in self._entries])\n   497→\n   498→    async def compensate(self) -> None:\n   499→        if not self._published:\n   500→            return\n   501→        # Events are immutable; Idempotency key for de-duplication across retries\n    status: EntryStatus = field(default=EntryStatus.PENDING)\n    created_at: datetime = field(\n        default_factory=lambda: datetime.now(timezone.utc), init=False\n    )\n    metadata: Dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)\n\n    # -- Behaviors ---------------------------------------------------------- #\n\n    def commit(self) -> None:\n        if self.status is not EntryStatus.PENDING:\n            raise LedgerError(f\"Cannot commit entry {self.id}: status = {self.status}\")\n        self.status = EntryStatus.COMMITTED\n\n    def reverse(self, reason: str, *, failed: bool = False) -> None:\n        if self.status is EntryStatus.COMMITTED:\n            self.status = EntryStatus.REVERSED if not failed else EntryStatus.FAILED\n            self.metadata[\"reverse_reason\"] = reason\n        else:\n            raise LedgerError(f\"Cannot reverse entry {self.id}: status = {self.status}\")\n\n\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #\n# Auxiliary helpers                                                           #\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #\n\n\nclass AsyncLedgerTxn:\n    \"\"\"\n    Async context manager returned by repository.transaction().\n    \"\"\"\n\n    async def __aenter__(self) -> \"AsyncLedgerTxn\":\n        return self\n\n    async def __aexit__(\n        self,\n        exc_type: Optional[Type[BaseException]],\n        exc_val: Optional[BaseException],\n        exc_tb: Optional[TracebackType],\n    ) -> Optional[bool]:\n        # pylint: disable=broad-except\n            logger.error(\"Saga step failed; starting compensation\", exc_info=exc)\n            await self._compensate()\n            raise SagaCommitError(\"Saga aborted due to failure\") from exc\n\n    async def _compensate(self) -> None:\n        for step in reversed(self._completed):\n            with contextlib.suppress(Exception):\n                await step.compensate()\n\n\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #\n# Ledger service                                                              #\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #\n\n\nclass LedgerService:\n    \"\"\"\n    Application-level facade responsible for recording double-entry\n    transactions and ensuring eventual consistency through sagas and events.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(\n        self,\n        repo: AbstractLedgerRepository,\n        publisher: AbstractEventPublisher,\n        fx_provider: AbstractFxRateProvider,\n    ) -> None:\n        self._repo = repo\n        self._publisher = publisher\n        self._fx = fx_provider\n\n    # --------------------------------------------------------------------- #\n    # Public API                                                             #\n    # --------------------------------------------------------------------- #\n\n    async def transfer(\n        self,\n        *,\n        origin_wallet_id: uuid.UUID,\n        dest_wallet_id: uuid.UUID,\n        amount: Decimal,\n        currency: Currency,\n        external_txn_id: str,\n        description: str = \"\",\n        metadata: Optional[Mapping[str, Any]] = None,\n    ) -> Tuple[LedgerEntry, LedgerEntry]:\n        \"\"\"\n        Orchestrate a double-entry transfer between two wallets.\n\n        1. Validate idempotency.\n        2. Check available funds.\n        3. Assemble **pending** ledger entries.\n        4. Execute Saga:\n           a. Persist entries atomically.\n           b. Commit entries.\n        5. Publish events.\n\n        Returns\n        -------\n        Tuple[LedgerEntry, LedgerEntry]\n            `(debit_entry, credit_entry)`\n        \"\"\"\n        if await self._repo.fetch_entry_by_external_id(external_txn_id):\n            logger.info(\"Duplicate external_txn_id=%s. Fast-returning.\", external_txn_id)\n            raise DuplicateEntryError(f\"Transaction {external_txn_id} already processed\")\n\n        money = Money(Decimal(amount), currency)\n\n        # Step 1 – ensure origin wallet has sufficient funds\n        await self._assert_sufficient_balance(origin_wallet_id, money)\n\n        # Step 2 – create *pending* ledger entries\n        debit_entry = LedgerEntry(\n            id=uuid.uuid4(),\n            wallet_id=origin_wallet_id,\n            entry_type=EntryType.DEBIT,\n            money=money,\n            external_txn_id=external_txn_id,\n            metadata={\"description\": description, **(metadata or {})},\n        )\n        credit_entry = LedgerEntry(\n            id=uuid.uuid4(),\n            wallet_id=dest_wallet_id,\n            entry_type=EntryType.CREDIT,\n            money=money,\n            external_txn_id=external_txn_id,\n            metadata={\"description\": description, **(metadata or {})},\n        )\n\n        # Step 3 – wrap in a local saga to ensure durability + commit\n        saga_steps: List[SagaStep] = [\n            _PersistEntriesStep(self._repo, debit_entry, credit_entry),\n            _CommitEntriesStep(self._repo, debit_entry, credit_entry),\n            _PublishEventsStep(self._publisher, debit_entry, credit_entry),\n        ]\n        saga = SagaCoordinator(saga_steps)\n\n        await saga.execute()\n        logger.info(\n            \"Transfer committed: %s -> %s amount=%s %s\",\n            origin_wallet_id,\n            dest_wallet_id,\n            money.amount,\n            money.currency,\n        )\n        return debit_entry, credit_entry\n\n    async def get_balances(\n        self, wallet_id: uuid.UUID, *, in_currency: Optional[Currency] = None\n    ) -> Mapping[Currency, Money]:\n        \"\"\"\n        Aggregate current balances per currency for a wallet. If\n        `in_currency` is provided, convert all balances to that currency.\n        \"\"\"\n        balances = await self._repo.list_wallet_balance(wallet_id)\n        if in_currency is None:\n            return balances\n\n        async def _convert(m: Money) -> Money:\n            if m.currency == in_currency:\n                return m\n            return await self._fx.convert(m, in_currency)\n\n        tasks = [asyncio.create_task(_convert(m)) for m in balances.values()]\n        converted = await asyncio.gather(*tasks)\n        return {in_currency: sum((m.amount for m in converted), Decimal(\"0\"))}\n\n    # --------------------------------------------------------------------- #\n    # Internal helpers                                                      #\n    # --------------------------------------------------------------------- #\n\n    async def _assert_sufficient_balance(\n        self, wallet_id: uuid.UUID, debit_amount: Money\n    ) -> None:\n        balances = await self._repo.list_wallet_balance(wallet_id)\n        available = balances.get(debit_amount.currency, Money(Decimal(\"0\"), debit_amount.currency))\n        if available.amount < debit_amount.amount:\n            raise InsufficientFundsError(\n                f\"Wallet {wallet_id} has {available.amount}, \"\n                f\"needs {debit_amount.amount} available.amount,\n            debit_amount.amount,\n        )\n\n\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #\n# Saga Steps                                                                  #\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #\n\n\nclass _PersistEntriesStep:\n    def __init__(self, repo: AbstractLedgerRepository, self._repo.save_entries(self._entries)\n        logger.debug(\"Ledger entries persisted: %s\", [e.id for e in self._entries])\n\n    async def compensate(self) -> None:\n        async with failed=True,\n            )\n        logger.debug(\"Persist saga compensation applied to entries: %s\", [e.id for e in self._entries])\n\n\nclass _CommitEntriesStep:\n    def __init__(self, repo: logger.debug(\"Ledger entries committed: %s\", [e.id for e in self._entries])\n\n    async def compensate(self) -> None:\n        async with self._repo.transaction():\n            await self._repo.mark_reversed(\n                [e.id for e in self._entries],\n                reason=\"Saga compensation post commit\",\n                failed=True,\n            )\n        logger.debug(\"Commit saga compensation executed for entries: %s\", [e.id for e in self._entries])\n\n\nclass _PublishEventsStep:\n    \"\"\"\n    Final saga step that broadcasts domain events. This step is intentionally\n    placed last so that consumers only see COMMITTED entries.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, publisher: AbstractEventPublisher, *entries: LedgerEntry):\n        self._publisher = publisher\n        self._entries = entries\n        self._published: bool = False\n\n    async def invoke(self) -> None:\n        tasks = [\n            self._publisher.publish(\n                \"ledger.entry.committed\",\n                {\n                    \"entry_id\": str(e.id),\n                    \"wallet_id\": str(e.wallet_id),\n                    \"type\": e.entry_type,\n                    \"amount\": str(e.money.amount),\n                    \"currency\": e.money.currency,\n                    \"external_txn_id\": e.external_txn_id,\n                    \"timestamp\": e.created_at.isoformat(),\n                },\n            )\n            for e in self._entries\n        ]\n        await asyncio.gather(*tasks)\n        self._published = True\n        logger.debug(\"Ledger events published for entries: %s\", [e.id for e in self._entries])\n\n    async def compensate(self) -> None:\n        if not self._published:\n            return\n        # Events are immutable; to compensate we emit a \"reversed\" event\n        tasks = [\n            self._publisher.publish(\n                \"ledger.entry.reversed\",\n                {\"entry_id\": str(e.id), \"reason\": \"Saga compensation\"},\n            )\n            for e in self._entries\n        ]\n        await asyncio.gather(*tasks)\n        logger.debug(\"Compensation events emitted for entries: %s\", [e.id for e in self._entries])\n```","numLines":511,"startLine":1,"totalLines":511}}}

__init__(\n        self,\n        rule_sources: Iterable[pathlib.Path | Dict[str, Any]],\n        *,\n        publisher: Optional[ComplianceEventPublisher] = None,\n    ) -> None:\n        \"\"\"\n        Parameters\n        ----------\n        rule_sources:\n            Collection of YAML/JSON files *or* pre-parsed rule dictionaries\n            from which rules will be loaded at start-up.  Hot reloading can be\n            achieved by constructing a new engine instance.\n        publisher:\n            Event publisher; if ``None``, a default stub is used.\n        \"\"\"\n        self._lock = threading.RLock()\n        self._rules = []\n        self._publisher = publisher or ComplianceEventPublisher()\n        self._load_rules(rule_sources)\n\n    # --------------------------------------------------------------------- #\n    #  Public interface                                                     #\n    # --------------------------------------------------------------------- #\n\n    def evaluate(\n        self, *, tx: Transaction, user: UserProfile, extra_ctx: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None\n    ) -> List[RuleResult]:\n        \"\"\"\n        Evaluate all active rules against the transaction & user context.\n\n        Returns a list of RuleResult items sorted by rule priority.\n        \"\"\"\n        ctx = {\n            \"transaction\": tx,\n            \"user\": user,\n            **(extra_ctx or {}),\n        }\n        LOGGER.debug(\"Starting rule evaluation for tx=%s\", tx.tx_id)\n        results: List[RuleResult] = []\n        with self._lock:\n            for rule in sorted(self._rules, key=lambda r: r.priority, reverse=True):\n                if not rule.enabled:\n                    continue\n\n                if rule._compiled_conditions is None:\n                    rule._compiled_conditions = [\n                        _compile_condition(expr) for expr in rule.conditions\n                    ]\n\n                matched = all(predicate(**ctx) for predicate in rule._compiled_conditions)\n\n                if matched:\n                    result = self._on_rule_match(rule=rule, ctx=ctx)\n                    results.append(result)\n                    LOGGER.info(\n                        \"Rule matched: '%s' (decision=%s, risk=%s) on tx=%s\",\n                        rule.name,\n                        result.decision,\n                        result.risk_level,\n                        tx.tx_id,\n                    )\n                else:\n                    results.append(\n                        RuleResult(\n                            rule_name=rule.name,\n                            matched=False,\n                        )\n                    )\n\n        return results\n\n    # --------------------------------------------------------------------- #\n    #  Internal helpers                                                     #\n    # --------------------------------------------------------------------- #\n\n    def _on_rule_match(self, *, rule: Rule, ctx: Dict[str, Any]) -> RuleResult:\n        \"\"\"\n        Build RuleResult, publish event, and return it.\n        \"\"\"\n        decision_str = rule.on_match.get(\"decision\", \"MANUAL_REVIEW\").upper()\n        risk_str = rule.on_match.get(\"risk_level\", \"MEDIUM\").upper()\n\n        try:\n            decision = Decision[decision_str]\n        except KeyError:\n            decision = Decision.MANUAL_REVIEW\n\n        try:\n            risk = RiskLevel[risk_str]\n        except KeyError:\n            risk = RiskLevel.MEDIUM\n\n        result = RuleResult(\n            rule_name=rule.name,\n            matched=True,\n            decision=decision,\n            risk_level=risk,\n            metadata={k: v for k, v in rule.on_match.items() if k not in {\"decision\", \"risk_level\"}},\n        )\n\n        event_payload = {\n            \"event_id\": f\"cmpl-{int(time()*1000)}\",\n            \"timestamp\": int(time()),\n            \"rule_name\": rule.name,\n            \"tx_id\": ctx[\"transaction\"].tx_id,\n            \"user_id\": ctx[\"user\"].user_id,\n            \"decision\": decision.name,\n            \"risk_level\": risk.name,\n            \"metadata\": result.metadata,\n        }\n\n        self._publisher.publish(event_payload)\n        return result\n\n    def _load_rules(self, sources: Iterable[pathlib.Path | Dict[str, Any]]) -> None:\n        \"\"\"\n        Populate in-memory rule list from given sources.\n\n        Supports JSON (.json) or YAML (.yml/.yaml) files, as well as raw dicts.\n        \"\"\"\n        import yaml  # Locally scoped import to avoid dependency if not needed.\n\n        for src in sources:\n            try:\n                if isinstance(src, pathlib.Path):\n                    if not src.exists():\n                        raise FileNotFoundError(src)\n                    with src.open(\"rt\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as fp:\n                        if src.suffix.lower() in {\".yaml\", \".yml\"}:\n                            raw_rules = yaml.safe_load(fp)\n                        elif src.suffix.lower() == \".json\":\n                            raw_rules = json.load(fp)\n                        else:\n                            LOGGER.warning(\"Unsupported rule file type: %s\", src)\n                            continue\n                elif isinstance(src, dict):\n                    raw_rules = [src]  # Single rule dict\n                else:\n                    LOGGER.warning(\"Unknown rule source: %s\", src)\n                    continue\n\n                for raw in raw_rules:\n                    rule = self._build_rule(raw)\n                    self._rules.append(rule)\n                    LOGGER.debug(\"Loaded rule: %s\", rule.name)\n\n            except Exception as exc:  # pylint: disable=broad-except\n                LOGGER.error(\"Failed to load rule from %s: %s\", src, exc, exc_info=True)\n\n        if not self._rules:\n            raise RuntimeError(\"No rules loaded – risk compliance engine cannot start!\")\n\n    @staticmethod\n    def _build_rule(data: Dict[str, Any]) -> Rule:\n        \"\"\"Validate and convert raw dict to Rule dataclass.\"\"\"\n        required_fields = {\"name\", \"description\", \"conditions\"}\n        missing = required_fields - data.keys()\n        if missing:\n            raise ValueError(f\"Rule definition missing fields: {missing}\")\n\n        return Rule(\n            name=data[\"name\"],\n            description=data[\"description\"],\n            priority=int(data.get(\"priority\", 50)),\n            conditions=tuple(map(str.strip, data[\"conditions\"])),\n            on_match=dict(data.get(\"actions\") or {}),\n            enabled=bool(data.get(\"enabled\", True)),\n        )\n\n\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #\n#  Example usage (will be stripped during package build)                      #\n# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":  # pragma: no cover\n    logging.basicConfig(level=logging.DEBUG)\n\n    # Example rule definitions defined inline for demonstration purposes.\n    RULES = [\n        {\n            \"name\": \"HighAmountCrossBorder\",\n            \"description\": \"Flag cross-border transfers over $10k\",\n            \"priority\": 90,\n            \"conditions\": [\n                \"transaction.amount > 10_000\",\n                \"transaction.country != user.country\",\n            ],\n            \"actions\": {\n                \"risk_level\": \"HIGH\",\n                \"decision\": \"MANUAL_REVIEW\",\n                \"alert_code\": \"AML-HACB-001\",\n            },\n        },\n        {\n            \"name\": \"UnverifiedUserLargeTx\",\n            \"description\": \"Reject large transactions from non-KYC verified users\",\n            \"priority\": 95,\n            \"conditions\": [\n                \"user.kyc_status != 'VERIFIED'\",\n                \"transaction.amount >= 5_000\",\n            ],\n            \"actions\": {\n                \"risk_level\": \"CRITICAL\",\n                \"decision\": \"REJECT\",\n                \"alert_code\": \"KYC-UVU-002\",\n            },\n        },\n    ]\n\n    engine = RuleEngine(rule_sources=[RULES[0], RULES[1]])\n\n    result = engine.evaluate(\n        tx=Transaction(\n            tx_id=\"TX123456\",\n            pod_id=\"POD987\",\n            user_id=\"USER42\",\n            amount=12_500,\n            currency=\"USD\",\n            country=\"US\",\n            created_at=time(),\n        ),\n        user=UserProfile(\n            user_id=\"USER42\",\n            kyc_status=\"PENDING\",\n            reputation_score=42.0,\n            country=\"CA\",\n            is_pep=False,\n        ),\n    )\n\n    for r in result:\n        print(r)\n```","numLines":467,"startLine":1,"totalLines":467}}}

  * Graph heuristics – CrowdPod network trust, follower endorsements\n      * Machine-learning ensembles\n    \"\"\"\n\n    HIGH_RISK_CURRENCIES = {\"NGN\", \"KHR\", \"LAK\"}  # Example high-risk fiat\n\n    def score(\n        self,\n        amount: float,\n        currency_code: str,\n        user_reputation: float,\n        pod_reputation: float,\n    ) -> float:\n        \"\"\"\n        Returns a float in the range [0, 1] representing risk probability.\n        \"\"\"\n        base = min(1.0, amount / 10_000)  # scale amount to 0-1\n        currency_modifier = 0.15 if currency_code in self.HIGH_RISK_CURRENCIES else 0\n        reputation_modifier = (1 - user_reputation) * 0.4 + (1 - pod_reputation) * 0.2\n        score = min(1.0, base + currency_modifier + reputation_modifier)\n reputation_modifier,\n        )\n        return score\n\n    def decide(self, score: float) -> AssessmentDecision:\n        if score < 0.5:\n            return AssessmentDecision.APPROVED\n        if Inject default type.\n    EVENT_REGISTRY[event_type] = cls\n    return cls\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------#\n# Example Event Definitions                                                  #\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------#\n@register_event\nclass CrowdPodCreated(BaseEvent):\n    crowdpod_id: str = Field(..., min_length=12, max_length=64)\n    owner_user_id: str = Field(..., min_length=12, max_length=64)\n    name: str = Field(..., min_length=1, max_length=120)\n    currency: str = Field(..., regex=r\"^[A-Z]{3}$\")\n    initial_balance_minor: int = Field(0, ge=0)\n\n\n@register_event\nclass FundDeposited(BaseEvent):\n    crowdpod_id: str = Field(..., min_length=12, max_length=64)\n    deposit_txn_id: str = Field(..., min_length=12, max_length=64)\n    amount_minor: int = Field(..., gt=0)\n    currency: str = Field(..., regex=r\"^[A-Z]{3}$\")\n\n\n@register_event\nclass FundWithdrawn(BaseEvent):\n    crowdpod_id: str = Field(..., min_length=12, max_length=64)\n    withdrawal_txn_id: str = Field(..., min_length=12, max_length=64)\n    amount_minor: int = Field(..., gt=0)\n    currency: str = Field(..., regex=r\"^[A-Z]{3}$\")\n    risk_assessment_score: float = Field(..., ge=0.0, le=1.0)\n\n\n@register_event\nclass KYCVerified(BaseEvent):\n    user_id: str = Field(..., min_length=12, max_length=64)\n    verification_reference: str = Field(..., min_length=12, max_length=64)\n    provider: str = Field(..., min_length=2, max_length=64)\n    # Schema evolution example ----------------------------------------------\n    schema_version: int = 2  # Incremented due to breaking change\n\n    def upgrade(self) -> BaseEvent:  # pragma: no cover\n        \"\"\"\n        Downgrade path for legacy consumers pinned to ``schema_version=1``.\n        \"\"\"\n        if self.schema_version == 1:\n            return self\n        # Simulated downgrade transformation.\n        return self.copy(update={\"schema_version\": 1, \"provider\": \"legacy\"})\n\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------#\n# Dynamic Discovery (optional)                                               #\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------#\ndef _auto_import_events() -> None:\n    \"\"\"\n    Eagerly import event modules declared under *crowdpay_connect.events*.\n    Allows plug-ins to register themselves without direct imports.\n    \"\"\"\n    pkg = \"crowdpay_connect.events\"\n    try:\n        package_path = Path(importlib.import_module(pkg).__file__).parent  # {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-5-20251101","id":"msg_01QRkqS23qUm9752PYJcmidL","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01EZi3LP22gDD3vwfYwFCvbm","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/logs/agent/solution.md","content":"# INSTANT_SETTLEMENT_PROPOSAL.md\n\n# CrowdPay Connect — Instant Settlement Feature\n## Architectural Proposal\n\n**Author:** Lead Architect\n**Date:** 2026-02-02\n**Status:** Draft\n**Version:** 1.0\n\n---\n\n## Table of Contents\n\n1. [Executive Summary](#1-executive-summary)\n2. [Analysis of the Current Architecture](#2-analysis-of-the-current-architecture)\n3. [Proposed Solution](#3-proposed-solution)\n4. [Failure Handling & Compensation Strategy](#4-failure-handling--compensation-strategy)\n5. [Sequence Diagram](#5-sequence-diagram)\n6. [Implementation Considerations](#6-implementation-considerations)\n7. [References](#7-references)\n\n---\n\n## 1. Executive Summary\n\nThis proposal outlines the architectural design for integrating the \"Instant Settlement\" premium feature into CrowdPay Connect. The feature will enable select premium users to have their transactions settled in near real-time via a new third-party provider called **InstaPay**, contrasting with the standard T+2 asynchronous settlement process.\n\n**Key Design Decision:** We propose a **Strategy Pattern** to segregate the instant settlement flow from the existing asynchronous Saga-based payment workflow. This approach preserves the integrity of the current architecture while introducing a parallel, optimized path for premium transactions.\n\n---\n\n## 2. Analysis of the Current Architecture\n\n### 2.1 Architectural Patterns Overview\n\nCrowdPay Connect employs several sophisticated architectural patterns for handling payments:\n\n| Pattern | Purpose | Key Components |\n|---------|---------|----------------|\n| **Saga Pattern** | Distributed transaction orchestration | `payment_saga.py`, `saga_coordinator.py` |\n| **Event Sourcing** | Immutable audit trail & state reconstruction | Event Store (PostgreSQL), Kafka topics |\n| **CQRS** | Separate read/write concerns | Command handlers, Read projections |\n| **Double-Entry Ledger** | Financial integrity | `ledger.py` in wallet_service |\n\n### 2.2 Standard Payment Settlement Flow\n\nThe current payment flow is orchestrated by the `PaymentSaga` class in `services/transaction_service/app/sagas/payment_saga.py`. The saga consists of 6 ordered steps:\n\n```\n1. KYC Verification      → kyc_service.verify_customer()\n2. Risk Assessment       → risk_service.assess_payment()\n3. Debit Payer           → wallet_service.debit()  [compensable]\n4. Currency Conversion   → forex_service.convert() [compensable]\n5. Credit CrowdPod       → wallet_service.credit() [compensable]\n6. Settlement            → settlement_service.settle_transaction() [compensable]\n```\n\n**File Reference:** `services/transaction_service/app/sagas/payment_saga.py:238-273`\n\nThe saga orchestrator in `services/transaction_service/app/events/saga_coordinator.py:222-300` manages:\n- Step execution with timeout handling (default 5-minute TTL)\n- Automatic compensation in reverse order on failure\n- State persistence and recovery via `SagaRepository`\n- Event publication for observability\n\n### 2.3 Saga Coordinator Mechanics\n\nThe `SagaCoordinator` class (`saga_coordinator.py:222`) provides:\n\n```python\nclass SagaCoordinator:\n    async def execute(self, saga_id: str) -> Saga:\n        # For each step:\n        #   1. Check expiration\n        #   2. Execute action\n        #   3. Persist state\n        #   4. Publish update event\n        # On failure: run compensations in reverse order\n```\n\n**Key Characteristics:**\n- **Asynchronous execution**: Steps are awaited sequentially\n- **Persistence**: Every state transition is persisted (`SagaRepository`)\n- **Recovery**: Sagas can be resumed after crashes\n- **Compensation**: Automatic rollback via `_run_compensations()`\n\n**File Reference:** `services/transaction_service/app/events/saga_coordinator.py:262-300`\n\n### 2.4 Wallet Service Ledger Architecture\n\nThe ledger in `services/wallet_service/app/core/ledger.py` implements:\n\n- **Immutable double-entry bookkeeping**: Every transfer creates paired DEBIT/CREDIT entries\n- **Event-sourced updates**: Ledger entries transition through states (PENDING → COMMITTED → REVERSED)\n- **Event publication**: Changes are broadcast via `AbstractEventPublisher`\n\n**Key Design Point:** The ledger does NOT expose direct mutation APIs. All state changes are event-driven:\n\n```python\n# From ledger.py:305-375 - LedgerService.transfer()\nasync def transfer(self, ...):\n    # 1. Idempotency check\n    # 2. Balance validation\n    # 3. Create PENDING entries\n    # 4. Execute local Saga: Persist → Commit → Publish Events\n```\n\n**File Reference:** `services/wallet_service/app/core/ledger.py:285-375`\n\n### 2.5 Wallet Event Consumer\n\nThe `EventConsumer` in `services/wallet_service/app/events/consumer.py` handles:\n- Event deserialization (CloudEvents format)\n- Dispatching to domain handlers\n- At-least-once delivery with Kafka commit management\n\n**File Reference:** `services/wallet_service/app/events/consumer.py:278-327`\n\n### 2.6 Risk & Compliance Service\n\nThe `RuleEngine` in `services/risk_compliance_service/app/core/rule_engine.py:224-304` provides:\n- Hot-swappable rule definitions (YAML/JSON)\n- Safe expression compilation (AST-validated)\n- Real-time transaction scoring\n- Decision output: `APPROVE`, `MANUAL_REVIEW`, or `REJECT`\n\nThe assessment API (`services/risk_compliance_service/app/api/v1/assessment.py:218-257`) is **asynchronous by design**:\n- Returns `202 Accepted` immediately\n- Executes scoring in background tasks\n- Publishes results via event bus\n\n**File Reference:** `services/risk_compliance_service/app/core/rule_engine.py:259-304`\n\n### 2.7 Why Synchronous Calls Inside the Saga Are an Anti-Pattern\n\nThe existing Saga pattern is **fundamentally asynchronous** and designed for:\n- Resilience against network partitions\n- Crash recovery via event replay\n- Loose coupling between services\n\n**Introducing a synchronous blocking call to InstaPay inside the existing `PaymentSaga` would:**\n\n1. **Violate the Saga contract**: Sagas expect compensable, idempotent steps—not blocking external API calls with unknown latency\n2. **Create a single point of failure**: A slow/unavailable InstaPay would block the entire payment flow\n3. **Break timeout semantics**: The 5-minute saga TTL doesn't account for external API SLAs\n4. **Compromise observability**: Event-driven tracing would lose visibility into the blocking call\n5. **Prevent proper compensation**: External settlement state is not easily reversible\n\n---\n\n## 3. Proposed Solution\n\n### 3.1 Strategy Pattern for Settlement Type Selection\n\nWe propose implementing a **Strategy Pattern** at the transaction creation entry point. A factory or conditional logic will select between:\n\n| Strategy | Trigger Condition | Flow Type |\n|----------|-------------------|-----------|\n| `StandardSettlementStrategy` | Default transactions | Existing Saga (async) |\n| `InstantSettlementStrategy` | Premium user + eligible transaction | New synchronous flow |\n\n**Selection Logic (Pseudo-code):**\n\n```python\n# In transaction_service - transaction creation handler\nclass SettlementStrategyFactory:\n    def create(self, transaction: Transaction, user: User) -> SettlementStrategy:\n        if user.is_premium and transaction.eligible_for_instant_settlement:\n            return InstantSettlementStrategy(\n                risk_service=self.risk_service,\n                wallet_service=self.wallet_service,\n                instapay_client=self.instapay_client,\n                event_bus=self.event_bus,\n            )\n        return StandardSettlementStrategy(\n            payment_saga=self.payment_saga,\n        )\n```\n\n### 3.2 InstantSettlementStrategy Architecture\n\nThe `InstantSettlementStrategy` will orchestrate a **synchronous, self-contained flow** that bypasses the traditional Saga:\n\n```python\nclass InstantSettlementStrategy:\n    \"\"\"\n    Synchronous settlement path for premium instant settlements.\n\n    This strategy does NOT use the existing PaymentSaga to avoid mixing\n    asynchronous event-driven patterns with blocking external calls.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    async def execute(self, transaction: Transaction) -> SettlementResult:\n        # Phase 1: Pre-authorization risk check (SYNCHRONOUS)\n        risk_decision = await self._sync_risk_assessment(transaction)\n        if risk_decision != Decision.APPROVE:\n            raise SettlementRejectedError(risk_decision)\n\n        # Phase 2: Reserve funds in wallet (SYNCHRONOUS)\n        reservation_id = await self._reserve_funds(transaction)\n\n        try:\n            # Phase 3: Call InstaPay API (SYNCHRONOUS with timeout)\n            instapay_result = await self._call_instapay(transaction, reservation_id)\n\n            # Phase 4: Publish success event (for wallet_service to finalize)\n            await self._publish_instant_settlement_succeeded(\n                transaction, reservation_id, instapay_result\n            )\n\n            return SettlementResult(status=SUCCESS, external_ref=instapay_result.ref)\n\n        except InstaPayAPIError as e:\n            # Compensation: Release reserved funds\n            await self._publish_release_reserved_funds(transaction, reservation_id)\n            raise SettlementFailedError(e) from e\n```\n\n### 3.3 Interaction with External InstaPay API\n\n**Recommendation:** The InstaPay call should be **synchronous with strict timeout controls**.\n\n| Configuration | Value | Rationale |\n|---------------|-------|-----------|\n| Connection timeout | 3 seconds | Fail fast on network issues |\n| Read timeout | 10 seconds | Allow for processing time |\n| Retry attempts | 2 | Minimize user wait, but allow retry |\n| Circuit breaker | Yes | Prevent cascade failures |\n\n**Integration Pattern:**\n\n```python\nclass InstaPayClient:\n    def __init__(self, circuit_breaker: CircuitBreaker):\n        self._breaker = circuit_breaker\n        self._client = httpx.AsyncClient(timeout=httpx.Timeout(\n            connect=3.0,\n            read=10.0,\n        ))\n\n    async def settle_instant(self, request: InstaPayRequest) -> InstaPayResponse:\n        async with self._breaker:\n            response = await self._client.post(\n                f\"{INSTAPAY_BASE_URL}/v1/instant-settle\",\n                json=request.dict(),\n                headers={\"X-Idempotency-Key\": request.idempotency_key},\n            )\n            response.raise_for_status()\n            return InstaPayResponse.parse_obj(response.json())\n```\n\n**Why Synchronous for InstaPay?**\n\nUnlike the internal Saga which coordinates across owned services, InstaPay is:\n- A terminal operation (the \"commit\" point of settlement)\n- Owned by a third party with its own reliability guarantees\n- Expected to provide idempotent operations via idempotency keys\n\nA synchronous call with circuit breaker protection provides the best user experience for premium customers expecting near real-time settlement confirmation.\n\n### 3.4 Modifications to the Transaction Service\n\n**New Components:**\n\n| Component | Location | Purpose |\n|-----------|----------|---------|\n| `SettlementStrategy` (ABC) | `app/strategies/settlement.py` | Strategy interface |\n| `StandardSettlementStrategy` | `app/strategies/standard.py` | Wraps existing Saga |\n| `InstantSettlementStrategy` | `app/strategies/instant.py` | New synchronous flow |\n| `SettlementStrategyFactory` | `app/strategies/factory.py` | Strategy selection |\n| `InstaPayClient` | `app/clients/instapay.py` | InstaPay API client |\n\n**Existing Saga Unchanged:**\n\nThe key architectural decision is to **NOT modify the existing `PaymentSaga`**. The instant settlement flow is a separate, parallel path. This ensures:\n- Zero regression risk for existing payment flows\n- Clean separation of concerns\n- Independent scaling and monitoring\n\n### 3.5 Wallet Service Integration via Events\n\n**Critical Design Principle:** The `InstantSettlementStrategy` should NOT make direct API calls to the wallet service to update the ledger. Instead, it publishes events that the wallet service consumes.\n\n**New Events:**\n\n```python\n@register_event\nclass InstantSettlementSucceeded(BaseEvent):\n    \"\"\"\n    Published by InstantSettlementStrategy after successful InstaPay confirmation.\n    The wallet_service will consume this to finalize the ledger entry.\n    \"\"\"\n    type: str = \"instant_settlement.succeeded\"\n    transaction_id: str\n    reservation_id: str\n    payer_wallet_id: str\n    payee_wallet_id: str\n    amount_minor: int\n    currency: str\n    instapay_reference: str\n    settled_at: datetime\n\n\n@register_event\nclass InstantSettlementFailed(BaseEvent):\n    \"\"\"\n    Published when InstaPay call fails. Triggers fund release in wallet_service.\n    \"\"\"\n    type: str = \"instant_settlement.failed\"\n    transaction_id: str\n    reservation_id: str\n    payer_wallet_id: str\n    failure_reason: str\n    failed_at: datetime\n```\n\n**Wallet Service Consumer Update:**\n\nAdd new handlers to `services/wallet_service/app/events/consumer.py`:\n\n```python\n# New event types\nclass InstantSettlementSucceeded(Event):\n    type: str = \"instant_settlement.succeeded\"\n    data: Dict[str, Any]  # Contains reservation_id, amounts, etc.\n\nclass InstantSettlementFailed(Event):\n    type: str = \"instant_settlement.failed\"\n    data: Dict[str, Any]\n\n# New handlers\nasync def handle_instant_settlement_succeeded(event: InstantSettlementSucceeded) -> None:\n    \"\"\"\n    Finalize the reserved funds: commit the ledger entry from RESERVED → SETTLED.\n    \"\"\"\n    await ledger_service.finalize_reservation(\n        reservation_id=event.data[\"reservation_id\"],\n        settlement_reference=event.data[\"instapay_reference\"],\n    )\n\nasync def handle_instant_settlement_failed(event: InstantSettlementFailed) -> None:\n    \"\"\"\n    Release the reserved funds: revert the ledger entry from RESERVED → RELEASED.\n    \"\"\"\n    await ledger_service.release_reservation(\n        reservation_id=event.data[\"reservation_id\"],\n        reason=event.data[\"failure_reason\"],\n    )\n```\n\n**Why Event-Driven Updates?**\n\n1. **Preserves event-sourcing pattern**: The ledger remains immutable and event-driven\n2. **Maintains service boundaries**: No direct cross-service API calls for state mutations\n3. **Enables audit trail**: All state changes are recorded as events\n4. **Supports eventual consistency**: Wallet service processes at its own pace\n\n### 3.6 Risk & Compliance Service Changes\n\n**Requirement:** Instant settlements need a **synchronous** risk assessment path.\n\n**Current State:** The assessment API returns `202 Accepted` and processes asynchronously (see `assessment.py:218-257`).\n\n**Proposed Addition:** Add a new synchronous endpoint for instant settlement pre-authorization:\n\n```python\n@router.post(\n    \"/sync\",\n    response_model=SyncAssessmentOut,\n    status_code=status.HTTP_200_OK,\n    summary=\"Synchronous risk assessment for instant settlements\",\n)\nasync def create_sync_assessment(\n    payload: AssessmentIn,\n    engine: _RiskEngine = Depends(get_risk_engine),\n) -> SyncAssessmentOut:\n    \"\"\"\n    Synchronous risk assessment for premium instant settlement transactions.\n\n    This endpoint performs immediate risk scoring without background processing.\n    Use only for instant settlement flows where blocking is acceptable.\n    \"\"\"\n    # Direct synchronous scoring (no background task)\n    score = engine.score(\n        amount=float(payload.amount),\n        currency_code=payload.currency_code,\n        user_reputation=_fetch_user_reputation(payload.user_id),\n        pod_reputation=_fetch_pod_reputation(payload.pod_id),\n    )\n\n    # Apply instant-settlement specific rules (potentially stricter)\n    decision = engine.decide_instant(score)  # New method with stricter thresholds\n\n    return SyncAssessmentOut(\n        decision=decision,\n        score=score,\n        assessed_at=datetime.now(tz=timezone.utc),\n    )\n```\n\n**Instant-Specific Risk Rules:**\n\nThe risk engine should have **stricter thresholds** for instant settlements because:\n- Less time for manual review\n- Higher fraud risk due to speed\n- Premium feature = higher value transactions\n\n```python\n# In rule_engine.py - add instant settlement decision logic\ndef decide_instant(self, score: float) -> AssessmentDecision:\n    \"\"\"\n    Stricter decision thresholds for instant settlement transactions.\n    \"\"\"\n    if score < 0.3:  # Lower threshold than standard (0.5)\n        return AssessmentDecision.APPROVED\n    if score < 0.5:  # Was 0.75 for standard\n        return AssessmentDecision.HOLD  # Requires manual review, reject instant\n    return AssessmentDecision.DECLINED\n```\n\n---\n\n## 4. Failure Handling & Compensation Strategy\n\n### 4.1 Failure Scenarios\n\n| Scenario | Stage | Compensation Action |\n|----------|-------|---------------------|\n| Risk assessment rejects | Pre-reservation | None (no state changed) |\n| Wallet reservation fails | Reservation | None (no state changed) |\n| InstaPay API timeout | Post-reservation | Release reserved funds |\n| InstaPay API returns error | Post-reservation | Release reserved funds |\n| InstaPay API returns success, event publish fails | Post-settlement | Retry event publish (idempotent) |\n| Wallet service crashes before processing event | Post-settlement | Event replay on recovery |\n\n### 4.2 Compensation Flow\n\n```\nInstaPay Call Fails/Timeouts\n           │\n           ▼\n┌─────────────────────────────────────┐\n│  InstantSettlementStrategy          │\n│  catches InstaPayAPIError           │\n└───────────────┬─────────────────────┘\n                │\n                ▼\n┌─────────────────────────────────────┐\n│  Publish: InstantSettlementFailed   │\n│  - transaction_id                   │\n│  - reservation_id                   │\n│  - failure_reason                   │\n└───────────────┬─────────────────────┘\n                │\n                ▼\n┌─────────────────────────────────────┐\n│  Wallet Service Consumer            │\n│  handle_instant_settlement_failed() │\n└───────────────┬─────────────────────┘\n                │\n                ▼\n┌─────────────────────────────────────┐\n│  LedgerService.release_reservation()│\n│  - Mark entries as RELEASED         │\n│  - Restore available balance        │\n│  - Publish: ledger.funds.released   │\n└─────────────────────────────────────┘\n```\n\n### 4.3 Idempotency Guarantees\n\n**InstaPay API Call:**\n- Use `X-Idempotency-Key` header with unique `transaction_id`\n- InstaPay is expected to handle duplicate requests gracefully\n\n**Event Publishing:**\n- Events include unique `event_id` (ULID)\n- Wallet service consumer uses `external_txn_id` for deduplication\n- Kafka offsets committed only after successful processing\n\n**Ledger Operations:**\n- All ledger methods check `reservation_id` before state transitions\n- Duplicate requests return success (already processed)\n\n### 4.4 Compensation Implementation\n\n```python\n# In InstantSettlementStrategy\nasync def _compensate_reservation(\n    self,\n    transaction: Transaction,\n    reservation_id: str,\n    reason: str,\n) -> None:\n    \"\"\"\n    Publish compensation event for wallet service to release funds.\n    \"\"\"\n    event = InstantSettlementFailed(\n        transaction_id=transaction.id,\n        reservation_id=reservation_id,\n        payer_wallet_id=transaction.payer_wallet_id,\n        failure_reason=reason,\n        failed_at=datetime.now(timezone.utc),\n        correlation_id=transaction.correlation_id,\n    )\n\n    await self._event_bus.publish(\n        topic=\"instant_settlement.lifecycle\",\n        event=event,\n    )\n\n    logger.warning(\n        \"Compensation event published for instant settlement %s: %s\",\n        transaction.id,\n        reason,\n    )\n```\n\n---\n\n## 5. Sequence Diagram\n\nThe following Mermaid diagram illustrates the complete end-to-end flow for a successful Instant Settlement transaction:\n\n```mermaid\nsequenceDiagram\n    autonumber\n    actor TxSvc->>Wallet: POST /api/v1/wallets/{payer_id}/reserve\n        Wallet->>Wallet: Create PENDING ledger entry<br/>(double-entry: DEBIT)\n        Wallet-->>TxSvc: 200 OK {reservation_id: \"res_123\"}\n    end\n\n    %% Phase 3: InstaPay Settlement\n    rect rgb(230, 230, 255)\n        Note over TxSvc,InstaPay: Phase 3: External Settlement (Synchronous)\n        TxSvc->>InstaPay: POST /v1/instant-settle<br/>{amount, payer, payee, idempotency_key}\n        InstaPay->>InstaPay: Process settlement\n        InstaPay-->>TxSvc: 200 OK {reference: \"ip_456\", status: \"settled\"}\n API-->>User: Payment settled instantly!\n\n    %% Async Ledger Finalization\n    rect rgb(240, 240, 240)\n        Note over EventBus,WalletConsumer: Async: Ledger Finalization\n        EventBus-->>WalletConsumer: InstantSettlementSucceeded\n        WalletConsumer->>WalletConsumer: handle_instant_settlement_succeeded()\n        WalletConsumer->>WalletConsumer: LedgerService.finalize_reservation()<br/>PENDING → COMMITTED\n        WalletConsumer->>WalletConsumer: Create CREDIT entry for payee\n        WalletConsumer->>EventBus: Publish: ledger.entry.committed\n    end\n```\n\n### 5.1 Failure Scenario Diagram\n\n```mermaid\nsequenceDiagram\n    autonumber\n    actor User\n    participant TxSvc as Transaction Service<br/>(InstantSettlementStrategy)\n    participant Risk as Risk Service\n    participant Wallet as Wallet Service\n    participant InstaPay as InstaPay API\n    participant EventBus as Event default=Decimal(\"10000\"))\n```\n\n### 6.2 Monitoring & Alerting\n\n| Metric | Threshold | Action |\n|--------|-----------|--------|\n| InstaPay API latency p99 | > 8s | Alert on-call |\n| InstaPay error rate | > 5% | Circuit breaker + alert |\n| Compensation event volume | > 10/min | Investigate InstaPay issues |\n| Ledger PENDING entries age | > 5 min | Alert: stuck reservations |\n\n### 6.3 NULL UNIQUE,\n    payer_wallet_id UUID NOT NULL,\n    payee_wallet_id UUID NOT NULL,\n    amount_minor BIGINT NOT NULL,\n    currency VARCHAR(3) NOT NULL,\n    reservation_id UUID NOT NULL,\n settled_at TIMESTAMPTZ,\n    failed_at TIMESTAMPTZ,\n    failure_reason TEXT,\n\n    CONSTRAINT fk_reservation FOREIGN KEY (reservation_id)\n        REFERENCES ledger_entries(id)\n);\n\nCREATE INDEX idx_instant_settlements_status ON instant_settlements(status);\nCREATE INDEX idx_instant_settlements_created ON instant_settlements(created_at);\n```\n\n### 6.4 Testing Strategy\n\n1. **Unit Tests:**
