[Executive Summary](#1-executive-summary)\n2. [Analysis of the Current Architecture](#2-analysis-of-the-current-architecture)\n3. [Proposed Solution](#3-proposed-solution)\n4. [Failure Handling & Compensation Strategy](#4-failure-handling--compensation-strategy)\n5. [Sequence Diagram](#5-sequence-diagram)\n6. [Implementation Considerations](#6-implementation-considerations)\n7. [References](#7-references)\n\n---\n\n## 1. Executive Summary\n\nThis proposal outlines the architectural design for integrating the \"Instant Settlement\" premium feature into CrowdPay Connect. The feature will enable select premium users to have their transactions settled in near real-time via a new third-party provider called **InstaPay**, contrasting with the standard T+2 asynchronous settlement process.\n\n**Key Design Decision:** We propose a **Strategy Pattern** to segregate the instant settlement flow from the existing asynchronous Saga-based payment workflow. This approach preserves the integrity of the current architecture while introducing a parallel, optimized path for premium transactions.\n\n---\n\n## 2. Analysis of the Current Architecture\n\n### 2.1 Architectural Patterns Overview\n\nCrowdPay Connect employs several sophisticated architectural patterns for handling payments:\n\n| Pattern | Purpose | Key Components |\n|---------|---------|----------------|\n| **Saga Pattern** | Distributed transaction orchestration | `payment_saga.py`, `saga_coordinator.py` |\n| **Event Sourcing** | Immutable audit trail & state reconstruction | Event Store (PostgreSQL), Kafka topics |\n| **CQRS** | Separate read/write concerns | Command handlers, Read projections |\n| **Double-Entry Ledger** | Financial integrity | `ledger.py` in wallet_service |\n\n### 2.2 Standard Payment Settlement Flow\n\nThe current payment flow is orchestrated by the `PaymentSaga` class in `services/transaction_service/app/sagas/payment_saga.py`. The saga consists of 6 ordered steps:\n\n```\n1. KYC Verification      → kyc_service.verify_customer()\n2. Risk Assessment       → risk_service.assess_payment()\n3. Debit Payer           → wallet_service.debit()  [compensable]\n4. Currency Conversion   → forex_service.convert() [compensable]\n5. Credit CrowdPod       → wallet_service.credit() [compensable]\n6. Settlement            → settlement_service.settle_transaction() [compensable]\n```\n\n**File Reference:** `services/transaction_service/app/sagas/payment_saga.py:238-273`\n\nThe saga orchestrator in `services/transaction_service/app/events/saga_coordinator.py:222-300` manages:\n- Step execution with timeout handling (default 5-minute TTL)\n- Automatic compensation in reverse order on failure\n- State persistence and recovery via `SagaRepository`\n- Event publication for observability\n\n### 2.3 Saga Coordinator Mechanics\n\nThe `SagaCoordinator` class (`saga_coordinator.py:222`) provides:\n\n```python\nclass SagaCoordinator:\n    async def execute(self, saga_id: str) -> Saga:\n        # For each step:\n        #   1. Check expiration\n        #   2. Execute action\n        #   3. Persist state\n        #   4. Publish update event\n        # On failure: run compensations in reverse order\n```\n\n**Key Characteristics:**\n- **Asynchronous execution**: Steps are awaited sequentially\n- **Persistence**: Every state transition is persisted (`SagaRepository`)\n- **Recovery**: Sagas can be resumed after crashes\n- **Compensation**: Automatic rollback via `_run_compensations()`\n\n**File Reference:** `services/transaction_service/app/events/saga_coordinator.py:262-300`\n\n### 2.4 Wallet Service Ledger Architecture\n\nThe ledger in `services/wallet_service/app/core/ledger.py` implements:\n\n- **Immutable double-entry bookkeeping**: Every transfer creates paired DEBIT/CREDIT entries\n- **Event-sourced updates**: Ledger entries transition through states (PENDING → COMMITTED → REVERSED)\n- **Event publication**: Changes are broadcast via `AbstractEventPublisher`\n\n**Key Design Point:** The ledger does NOT expose direct mutation APIs. All state changes are event-driven:\n\n```python\n# From ledger.py:305-375 - LedgerService.transfer()\nasync def transfer(self, ...):\n    # 1. Idempotency check\n    # 2. Balance validation\n    # 3. Create PENDING entries\n    # 4. Execute local Saga: Persist → Commit → Publish Events\n```\n\n**File Reference:** `services/wallet_service/app/core/ledger.py:285-375`\n\n### 2.5 Wallet Event Consumer\n\nThe `EventConsumer` in `services/wallet_service/app/events/consumer.py` handles:\n- Event deserialization (CloudEvents format)\n- Dispatching to domain handlers\n- At-least-once delivery with Kafka commit management\n\n**File Reference:** `services/wallet_service/app/events/consumer.py:278-327`\n\n### 2.6 Risk & Compliance Service\n\nThe `RuleEngine` in `services/risk_compliance_service/app/core/rule_engine.py:224-304` provides:\n- Hot-swappable rule definitions (YAML/JSON)\n- Safe expression compilation (AST-validated)\n- Real-time transaction scoring\n- Decision output: `APPROVE`, `MANUAL_REVIEW`, or `REJECT`\n\nThe assessment API (`services/risk_compliance_service/app/api/v1/assessment.py:218-257`) is **asynchronous by design**:\n- Returns `202 Accepted` immediately\n- Executes scoring in background tasks\n- Publishes results via event bus\n\n**File Reference:** `services/risk_compliance_service/app/core/rule_engine.py:259-304`\n\n### 2.7 Why Synchronous Calls Inside the Saga Are an Anti-Pattern\n\nThe existing Saga pattern is **fundamentally asynchronous** and designed for:\n- Resilience against network partitions\n- Crash recovery via event replay\n- Loose coupling between services\n\n**Introducing a synchronous blocking call to InstaPay inside the existing `PaymentSaga` would:**\n\n1. **Violate the Saga contract**: Sagas expect compensable, idempotent steps—not blocking external API calls with unknown latency\n2. **Create a single point of failure**: A slow/unavailable InstaPay would block the entire payment flow\n3. **Break timeout semantics**: The 5-minute saga TTL doesn't account for external API SLAs\n4. **Compromise observability**: Event-driven tracing would lose visibility into the blocking call\n5. **Prevent proper compensation**: External settlement state is not easily reversible\n\n---\n\n## 3. Proposed Solution\n\n### 3.1 Strategy Pattern for Settlement Type Selection\n\nWe propose implementing a **Strategy Pattern** at the transaction creation entry point. A factory or conditional logic will select between:\n\n| Strategy | Trigger Condition | Flow Type |\n|----------|-------------------|-----------|\n| `StandardSettlementStrategy` | Default transactions | Existing Saga (async) |\n| `InstantSettlementStrategy` | Premium user + eligible transaction | New synchronous flow |\n\n**Selection Logic (Pseudo-code):**\n\n```python\n# In transaction_service - transaction creation handler\nclass SettlementStrategyFactory:\n    def create(self, transaction: Transaction, user: User) -> SettlementStrategy:\n        if user.is_premium and transaction.eligible_for_instant_settlement:\n            return InstantSettlementStrategy(\n                risk_service=self.risk_service,\n                wallet_service=self.wallet_service,\n                instapay_client=self.instapay_client,\n                event_bus=self.event_bus,\n            )\n        return StandardSettlementStrategy(\n            payment_saga=self.payment_saga,\n        )\n```\n\n### 3.2 InstantSettlementStrategy Architecture\n\nThe `InstantSettlementStrategy` will orchestrate a **synchronous, self-contained flow** that bypasses the traditional Saga:\n\n```python\nclass InstantSettlementStrategy:\n    \"\"\"\n    Synchronous settlement path for premium instant settlements.\n\n    This strategy does NOT use the existing PaymentSaga to avoid mixing\n    asynchronous event-driven patterns with blocking external calls.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    async def execute(self, transaction: Transaction) -> SettlementResult:\n        # Phase 1: Pre-authorization risk check (SYNCHRONOUS)\n        risk_decision = await self._sync_risk_assessment(transaction)\n        if risk_decision != Decision.APPROVE:\n            raise SettlementRejectedError(risk_decision)\n\n        # Phase 2: Reserve funds in wallet (SYNCHRONOUS)\n        reservation_id = await self._reserve_funds(transaction)\n\n        try:\n            # Phase 3: Call InstaPay API (SYNCHRONOUS with timeout)\n            instapay_result = await self._call_instapay(transaction, reservation_id)\n\n            # Phase 4: Publish success event (for wallet_service to finalize)\n            await self._publish_instant_settlement_succeeded(\n                transaction, reservation_id, instapay_result\n            )\n\n            return SettlementResult(status=SUCCESS, external_ref=instapay_result.ref)\n\n        except InstaPayAPIError as e:\n            # Compensation: Release reserved funds\n            await self._publish_release_reserved_funds(transaction, reservation_id)\n            raise SettlementFailedError(e) from e\n```\n\n### 3.3 Interaction with External InstaPay API\n\n**Recommendation:** The InstaPay call should be **synchronous with strict timeout controls**.\n\n| Configuration | Value | Rationale |\n|---------------|-------|-----------|\n| Connection timeout | 3 seconds | Fail fast on network issues |\n| Read timeout | 10 seconds | Allow for processing time |\n| Retry attempts | 2 | Minimize user wait, but allow retry |\n| Circuit breaker | Yes | Prevent cascade failures |\n\n**Integration Pattern:**\n\n```python\nclass InstaPayClient:\n    def __init__(self, circuit_breaker: CircuitBreaker):\n        self._breaker = circuit_breaker\n        self._client = httpx.AsyncClient(timeout=httpx.Timeout(\n            connect=3.0,\n            read=10.0,\n        ))\n\n    async def settle_instant(self, request: InstaPayRequest) -> InstaPayResponse:\n        async with self._breaker:\n            response = await self._client.post(\n                f\"{INSTAPAY_BASE_URL}/v1/instant-settle\",\n                json=request.dict(),\n                headers={\"X-Idempotency-Key\": request.idempotency_key},\n            )\n            response.raise_for_status()\n            return InstaPayResponse.parse_obj(response.json())\n```\n\n**Why Synchronous for InstaPay?**\n\nUnlike the internal Saga which coordinates across owned services, InstaPay is:\n- A terminal operation (the \"commit\" point of settlement)\n- Owned by a third party with its own reliability guarantees\n- Expected to provide idempotent operations via idempotency keys\n\nA synchronous call with circuit breaker protection provides the best user experience for premium customers expecting near real-time settlement confirmation.\n\n### 3.4 Modifications to the Transaction Service\n\n**New Components:**\n\n| Component | Location | Purpose |\n|-----------|----------|---------|\n| `SettlementStrategy` (ABC) | `app/strategies/settlement.py` | Strategy interface |\n| `StandardSettlementStrategy` | `app/strategies/standard.py` | Wraps existing Saga |\n| `InstantSettlementStrategy` | `app/strategies/instant.py` | New synchronous flow |\n| `SettlementStrategyFactory` | `app/strategies/factory.py` | Strategy selection |\n| `InstaPayClient` | `app/clients/instapay.py` | InstaPay API client |\n\n**Existing Saga Unchanged:**\n\nThe key architectural decision is to **NOT modify the existing `PaymentSaga`**. The instant settlement flow is a separate, parallel path. This ensures:\n- Zero regression risk for existing payment flows\n- Clean separation of concerns\n- Independent scaling and monitoring\n\n### 3.5 Wallet Service Integration via Events\n\n**Critical Design Principle:** The `InstantSettlementStrategy` should NOT make direct API calls to the wallet service to update the ledger. Instead, it publishes events that the wallet service consumes.\n\n**New Events:**\n\n```python\n@register_event\nclass InstantSettlementSucceeded(BaseEvent):\n    \"\"\"\n    Published by InstantSettlementStrategy after successful InstaPay confirmation.\n    The wallet_service will consume this to finalize the ledger entry.\n    \"\"\"\n    type: str = \"instant_settlement.succeeded\"\n    transaction_id: str\n    reservation_id: str\n    payer_wallet_id: str\n    payee_wallet_id: str\n    amount_minor: int\n    currency: str\n    instapay_reference: str\n    settled_at: datetime\n\n\n@register_event\nclass InstantSettlementFailed(BaseEvent):\n    \"\"\"\n    Published when InstaPay call fails. Triggers fund release in wallet_service.\n    \"\"\"\n    type: str = \"instant_settlement.failed\"\n    transaction_id: str\n    reservation_id: str\n    payer_wallet_id: str\n    failure_reason: str\n    failed_at: datetime\n```\n\n**Wallet Service Consumer Update:**\n\nAdd new handlers to `services/wallet_service/app/events/consumer.py`:\n\n```python\n# New event types\nclass InstantSettlementSucceeded(Event):\n    type: str = \"instant_settlement.succeeded\"\n    data: Dict[str, Any]  # Contains reservation_id, amounts, etc.\n\nclass InstantSettlementFailed(Event):\n    type: str = \"instant_settlement.failed\"\n    data: Dict[str, Any]\n\n# New handlers\nasync def handle_instant_settlement_succeeded(event: InstantSettlementSucceeded) -> None:\n    \"\"\"\n    Finalize the reserved funds: commit the ledger entry from RESERVED → SETTLED.\n    \"\"\"\n    await ledger_service.finalize_reservation(\n        reservation_id=event.data[\"reservation_id\"],\n        settlement_reference=event.data[\"instapay_reference\"],\n    )\n\nasync def handle_instant_settlement_failed(event: InstantSettlementFailed) -> None:\n    \"\"\"\n    Release the reserved funds: revert the ledger entry from RESERVED → RELEASED.\n    \"\"\"\n    await ledger_service.release_reservation(\n        reservation_id=event.data[\"reservation_id\"],\n        reason=event.data[\"failure_reason\"],\n    )\n```\n\n**Why Event-Driven Updates?**\n\n1. **Preserves event-sourcing pattern**: The ledger remains immutable and event-driven\n2. **Maintains service boundaries**: No direct cross-service API calls for state mutations\n3. **Enables audit trail**: All state changes are recorded as events\n4. **Supports eventual consistency**: Wallet service processes at its own pace\n\n### 3.6 Risk & Compliance Service Changes\n\n**Requirement:** Instant settlements need a **synchronous** risk assessment path.\n\n**Current State:** The assessment API returns `202 Accepted` and processes asynchronously (see `assessment.py:218-257`).\n\n**Proposed Addition:** Add a new synchronous endpoint for instant settlement pre-authorization:\n\n```python\n@router.post(\n    \"/sync\",\n    response_model=SyncAssessmentOut,\n    status_code=status.HTTP_200_OK,\n    summary=\"Synchronous risk assessment for instant settlements\",\n)\nasync def create_sync_assessment(\n    payload: AssessmentIn,\n    engine: _RiskEngine = Depends(get_risk_engine),\n) -> SyncAssessmentOut:\n    \"\"\"\n    Synchronous risk assessment for premium instant settlement transactions.\n\n    This endpoint performs immediate risk scoring without background processing.\n    Use only for instant settlement flows where blocking is acceptable.\n    \"\"\"\n    # Direct synchronous scoring (no background task)\n    score = engine.score(\n        amount=float(payload.amount),\n        currency_code=payload.currency_code,\n        user_reputation=_fetch_user_reputation(payload.user_id),\n        pod_reputation=_fetch_pod_reputation(payload.pod_id),\n    )\n\n    # Apply instant-settlement specific rules (potentially stricter)\n    decision = engine.decide_instant(score)  # New method with stricter thresholds\n\n    return SyncAssessmentOut(\n        decision=decision,\n        score=score,\n        assessed_at=datetime.now(tz=timezone.utc),\n    )\n```\n\n**Instant-Specific Risk Rules:**\n\nThe risk engine should have **stricter thresholds** for instant settlements because:\n- Less time for manual review\n- Higher fraud risk due to speed\n- Premium feature = higher value transactions\n\n```python\n# In rule_engine.py - add instant settlement decision logic\ndef decide_instant(self, score: float) -> AssessmentDecision:\n    \"\"\"\n    Stricter decision thresholds for instant settlement transactions.\n    \"\"\"\n    if score < 0.3:  # Lower threshold than standard (0.5)\n        return AssessmentDecision.APPROVED\n    if score < 0.5:  # Was 0.75 for standard\n        return AssessmentDecision.HOLD  # Requires manual review, reject instant\n    return AssessmentDecision.DECLINED\n```\n\n---\n\n## 4. Failure Handling & Compensation Strategy\n\n### 4.1 Failure Scenarios\n\n| Scenario | Stage | Compensation Action |\n|----------|-------|---------------------|\n| Risk assessment rejects | Pre-reservation | None (no state changed) |\n| Wallet reservation fails | Reservation | None (no state changed) |\n| InstaPay API timeout | Post-reservation | Release reserved funds |\n| InstaPay API returns error | Post-reservation | Release reserved funds |\n| InstaPay API returns success, event publish fails | Post-settlement | Retry event publish (idempotent) |\n| Wallet service crashes before processing event | Post-settlement | Event replay on recovery |\n\n### 4.2 Compensation Flow\n\n```\nInstaPay Call Fails/Timeouts\n           │\n           ▼\n┌─────────────────────────────────────┐\n│  InstantSettlementStrategy          │\n│  catches InstaPayAPIError           │\n└───────────────┬─────────────────────┘\n                │\n                ▼\n┌─────────────────────────────────────┐\n│  Publish: InstantSettlementFailed   │\n│  - transaction_id                   │\n│  - reservation_id                   │\n│  - failure_reason                   │\n└───────────────┬─────────────────────┘\n                │\n                ▼\n┌─────────────────────────────────────┐\n│  Wallet Service Consumer            │\n│  handle_instant_settlement_failed() │\n└───────────────┬─────────────────────┘\n                │\n                ▼\n┌─────────────────────────────────────┐\n│  LedgerService.release_reservation()│\n│  - Mark entries as RELEASED         │\n│  - Restore available balance        │\n│  - Publish: ledger.funds.released   │\n└─────────────────────────────────────┘\n```\n\n### 4.3 Idempotency Guarantees\n\n**InstaPay API Call:**\n- Use `X-Idempotency-Key` header with unique `transaction_id`\n- InstaPay is expected to handle duplicate requests gracefully\n\n**Event Publishing:**\n- Events include unique `event_id` (ULID)\n- Wallet service consumer uses `external_txn_id` for deduplication\n- Kafka offsets committed only after successful processing\n\n**Ledger Operations:**\n- All ledger methods check `reservation_id` before state transitions\n- Duplicate requests return success (already processed)\n\n### 4.4 Compensation Implementation\n\n```python\n# In InstantSettlementStrategy\nasync def _compensate_reservation(\n    self,\n    transaction: Transaction,\n    reservation_id: str,\n    reason: str,\n) -> None:\n    \"\"\"\n    Publish compensation event for wallet service to release funds.\n    \"\"\"\n    event = InstantSettlementFailed(\n        transaction_id=transaction.id,\n        reservation_id=reservation_id,\n        payer_wallet_id=transaction.payer_wallet_id,\n        failure_reason=reason,\n        failed_at=datetime.now(timezone.utc),\n        correlation_id=transaction.correlation_id,\n    )\n\n    await self._event_bus.publish(\n        topic=\"instant_settlement.lifecycle\",\n        event=event,\n    )\n\n    logger.warning(\n        \"Compensation event published for instant settlement %s: %s\",\n        transaction.id,\n        reason,\n    )\n```\n\n---\n\n## 5. Sequence Diagram\n\nThe following Mermaid diagram illustrates the complete end-to-end flow for a successful Instant Settlement transaction:\n\n```mermaid\nsequenceDiagram\n    autonumber\n    actor User\n    participant API as API Gateway\n    participant TxSvc as Transaction Service<br/>(InstantSettlementStrategy)\n    participant Risk as Risk & Compliance Service<br/>(/api/v1/assessment/sync)\n    participant Wallet as Wallet Service<br/>(LedgerService)\n    participant InstaPay as InstaPay API<br/>(External)\n    participant EventBus as Event Bus<br/>(Kafka)\n    participant WalletConsumer as Wallet Service<br/>(EventConsumer)\n\n    User->>API: POST /payments/instant<br/>{amount, payer_id, payee_id}\n    API->>TxSvc: Create instant settlement transaction\n\n    Note over TxSvc: SettlementStrategyFactory<br/>selects InstantSettlementStrategy\n\n    %% Phase 1: Risk Assessment\n    rect rgb(255, 240, 230)\n        Note over TxSvc,Risk: Phase 1: Synchronous Risk Assessment\n        TxSvc->>Risk: POST /api/v1/assessment/sync\n        Risk->>Risk: Execute instant settlement rules<br/>(stricter thresholds)\n        Risk-->>TxSvc: 200 OK {decision: APPROVED, score: 0.15}\n    end\n\n    %% Phase 2: Fund Reservation\n    rect rgb(230, 255, 230)\n        Note over TxSvc,Wallet: Phase 2: Reserve Funds\n        TxSvc->>Wallet: POST /api/v1/wallets/{payer_id}/reserve\n        Wallet->>Wallet: Create PENDING ledger entry<br/>(double-entry: DEBIT)\n        Wallet-->>TxSvc: 200 OK {reservation_id: \"res_123\"}\n    end\n\n    %% Phase 3: InstaPay Settlement\n    rect rgb(230, 230, 255)\n        Note over TxSvc,InstaPay: Phase 3: External Settlement (Synchronous)\n        TxSvc->>InstaPay: POST /v1/instant-settle<br/>{amount, payer, payee, idempotency_key}\n        InstaPay->>InstaPay: Process settlement\n        InstaPay-->>TxSvc: 200 OK {reference: \"ip_456\", status: \"settled\"}\n    end\n\n    %% Phase 4: Event Publication\n    rect rgb(255, 255, 230)\n        Note over TxSvc,EventBus: Phase 4: Publish Success Event\n        TxSvc->>EventBus: Publish: InstantSettlementSucceeded<br/>{transaction_id, reservation_id, instapay_ref}\n    end\n\n    %% Response to User\n    TxSvc-->>API: 200 OK {status: \"settled\", reference: \"ip_456\"}\n    API-->>User: Payment settled instantly!\n\n    %% Async Ledger Finalization\n    rect rgb(240, 240, 240)\n        Note over EventBus,WalletConsumer: Async: Ledger Finalization\n        EventBus-->>WalletConsumer: InstantSettlementSucceeded\n        WalletConsumer->>WalletConsumer: handle_instant_settlement_succeeded()\n        WalletConsumer->>WalletConsumer: LedgerService.finalize_reservation()<br/>PENDING → COMMITTED\n        WalletConsumer->>WalletConsumer: Create CREDIT entry for payee\n        WalletConsumer->>EventBus: Publish: ledger.entry.committed\n    end\n```\n\n### 5.1 Failure Scenario Diagram\n\n```mermaid\nsequenceDiagram\n    autonumber\n    actor User\n    participant TxSvc as Transaction Service<br/>(InstantSettlementStrategy)\n    participant Risk as Risk Service\n    participant Wallet as Wallet Service\n    participant InstaPay as InstaPay API\n    participant EventBus as Event Bus\n    participant WalletConsumer as Wallet Consumer\n\n    User->>TxSvc: Create instant settlement\n\n    TxSvc->>Risk: Sync risk assessment\n    Risk-->>TxSvc: APPROVED\n\n    TxSvc->>Wallet: Reserve funds\n    Wallet-->>TxSvc: reservation_id: \"res_123\"\n\n    TxSvc->>InstaPay: POST /v1/instant-settle\n\n    Note over InstaPay: API Error or Timeout\n    InstaPay--xTxSvc: 500 Error / Timeout\n\n    rect rgb(255, 220, 220)\n        Note over TxSvc,EventBus: Compensation Flow\n        TxSvc->>EventBus: Publish: InstantSettlementFailed<br/>{reservation_id, reason}\n    end\n\n    TxSvc-->>User: 500 Settlement failed\n\n    rect rgb(240, 240, 240)\n        Note over EventBus,WalletConsumer: Async: Fund Release\n        EventBus-->>WalletConsumer: InstantSettlementFailed\n        WalletConsumer->>WalletConsumer: handle_instant_settlement_failed()\n        WalletConsumer->>WalletConsumer: LedgerService.release_reservation()<br/>PENDING → RELEASED\n        WalletConsumer->>EventBus: Publish: ledger.funds.released\n    end\n```\n\n---\n\n## 6. Implementation Considerations\n\n### 6.1 Feature Flags\n\nImplement feature flags for gradual rollout:\n\n```python\nINSTANT_SETTLEMENT_ENABLED = env.bool(\"FEATURE_INSTANT_SETTLEMENT\", default=False)\nINSTANT_SETTLEMENT_USER_WHITELIST = env.list(\"INSTANT_SETTLEMENT_USERS\", default=[])\nINSTANT_SETTLEMENT_MAX_AMOUNT = env.decimal(\"INSTANT_SETTLEMENT_MAX_AMOUNT\", default=Decimal(\"10000\"))\n```\n\n### 6.2 Monitoring & Alerting\n\n| Metric | Threshold | Action |\n|--------|-----------|--------|\n| InstaPay API latency p99 | > 8s | Alert on-call |\n| InstaPay error rate | > 5% | Circuit breaker + alert |\n| Compensation event volume | > 10/min | Investigate InstaPay issues |\n| Ledger PENDING entries age | > 5 min | Alert: stuck reservations |\n\n### 6.3 Database Schema Changes\n\n**New table for instant settlement tracking:**\n\n```sql\nCREATE TABLE instant_settlements (\n    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),\n    transaction_id UUID NOT NULL UNIQUE,\n    payer_wallet_id UUID NOT NULL,\n    payee_wallet_id UUID NOT NULL,\n    amount_minor BIGINT NOT NULL,\n    currency VARCHAR(3) NOT NULL,\n    reservation_id UUID NOT NULL,\n    instapay_reference VARCHAR(64),\n    status VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'PENDING',\n    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),\n    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),\n    settled_at TIMESTAMPTZ,\n    failed_at TIMESTAMPTZ,\n    failure_reason TEXT,\n\n    CONSTRAINT fk_reservation FOREIGN KEY (reservation_id)\n        REFERENCES ledger_entries(id)\n);\n\nCREATE INDEX idx_instant_settlements_status ON instant_settlements(status);\nCREATE INDEX idx_instant_settlements_created ON instant_settlements(created_at);\n```\n\n### 6.4 Testing Strategy\n\n1. **Unit Tests:** Mock InstaPay client, verify strategy selection logic\n2. **Integration Tests:** Test full flow with InstaPay sandbox\n3. **Chaos Tests:** Inject InstaPay failures, verify compensation\n4. **Load Tests:** Verify system behavior under instant settlement load\n5. **Contract Tests:** Validate InstaPay API contract compliance\n\n---\n\n## 7.
